*Enes Yavuz Ugan,Fabian Retkowski,Yuka Ko,Thai-Binh Nguyen,Maike Züfle,Jan Niehues,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了KIT团队在BeTraC挑战赛轻量级赛道中的提交方案：通过可扩展的数据增强管道，将异构医学对话数据集统一为合成语音数据，并自动生成SOAP监督标签，实现语音基础模型的端到端语音到SOAP笔记生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的指令遵循能力推动了摘要任务的发展，其中临床协议（SOAP笔记）的抽取式摘要子任务备受关注，因为它能显著减少医护人员的工作负担。进一步自动化方向是直接从语音生成临床笔记，省去中间转录步骤，既缩短处理时间，又保留咳嗽等副语言线索（这些线索在基于转录的系统中可能丢失）。

Method: 核心贡献是一个可扩展的数据增强管道，具体包括：1）通过合成语音生成技术统一异构的医学对话数据集；2）自动生成SOAP监督标签；3）利用增强后的数据对语音基础模型进行鲁棒性适配，实现端到端的语音到SOAP生成。

Result: 论文为摘要阶段，尚未报告具体实验结果或基准测试成绩，主要贡献在于提出了数据增强管道和端到端语音到SOAP生成的技术路线。

Conclusion: 通过合成语音和自动SOAP标注的数据增强方法，能够使语音基础模型在端到端语音到SOAP笔记生成任务上实现鲁棒性适配，为临床文档自动化提供了减少中间转录环节的有效途径。

Abstract: With the advent of Large Language Models and its instruction following capabilities a promising application is the task of summarization. Within this domain of task the extractive sub-task of clinical protocolling has emerged as a topic of particular interest as it can significantly reduce the downtime and protocolling burden of health-care workers thus enabling them to focus on their core work helping humans. A further step towards automation is the direct generation of clinical notes from speech without intermediate transcripts, reducing processing time while preserving information such as coughing or other paralinguistic cues that may be lost in transcript-based systems. To this end, we present KIT's submission to this years BeTraC challenge in the lightweight track. Our main contribution is a scalable data augmentation pipeline that unifies heterogeneous medical dialogue datasets through synthetic speech generation and automatically generated SOAP supervision, enabling robust adaptation of a speech foundation model for end-to-end speech-to-SOAP generation.

</details>


### [32] [SteerCheck: Attribution Specificity and Alignment Leakage in Activation-Steering Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24335)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SteerCheck，一种预注册的归因审计框架，用于检验激活引导（activation steering）效果是否特异地归因于目标概念。通过对Qwen3-14B模型960次干预的精确重放，发现常见对照方法存在互补性局限：各向同性方向近正交，而符号随机化方向常保留显著的目标对齐。该"对齐泄漏"限制了条件随机化检验的区分能力。主Qwen完整门控结果为阴性，受保护尾部检验未通过所有对照族；独立数据上的迁移结果仅在部分场景成立。SteerCheck使有条件的、混合的结论可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导技术可以改变模型行为，但现有方法无法证明效果确实特异性地归因于预期概念（而非其他混杂因素）。作者希望建立一种严格的归因审计方法，将不同层次的主张（从目标外KL到语义刻板性）分离，并可靠区分真正的概念引导效应与无关对齐泄漏。

Method: SteerCheck是一套预注册的归因审计协议，包含：(1) 匹配目标外KL的对齐控制；(2) 分离均值、受保护尾部、极性、迁移和语义五类主张；(3) 精确重放960次Qwen3-14B干预，对比各向同性方向与符号随机化（sign-randomized）同构方向两类常见对照；(4) 引入构造诊断指标A和经验余弦分布的报告；(5) 结合人类三评者对开放生成结果的冻结评估。

Result: 符号随机化方向中有25.3%的抽取余弦超过0.5，且所有超过观测均值效应的抽取余弦均高于0.80；效应与符号随机化族内的签名余弦强相关（ρ=0.94）。主Qwen完整门控为阴性（受保护尾部失败于所有对照族）。连续边界迁移仅出现在Qwen中，准确率迁移在任何选定单元均未出现。DeepSeek中的通过（detox）比较器排除了范畴分离；所有名义通过都对Γ=1.10阈值敏感。深度求索（DeepSeek）支持事实修正，但Qwen不支持；自动评判器校准不良（宏F1=0.562），零宽语义结果仅为描述性。

Conclusion: 常见对照（各向同性方向和符号随机化方向）都是不完美的：前者过于窄近正交，后者存在对齐泄漏。对齐泄漏本身并不会使条件随机化检验失效，但它限制了对比较器能区分的假设空间，因此需要报告可交换性假设、构造诊断A和余弦分布作为标准实践。最终结论是有条件的和混合的：没有任何单一模型在所有主张上完全通过，这凸显了归因审计中区分主张类型和明确报告假设的重要性。

Abstract: Activation steering can change behaviour without establishing that the effect is specific to the intended concept. We introduce SteerCheck, a preregistered attribution audit that matches off-target KL and separates mean, protected-tail, polarity, transfer, and semantic claims. Exact replay of 960 Qwen3-14B interventions reveals complementary limits of common controls: isotropic directions occupy a narrow near-orthogonal region, whereas sign-randomized same-construction directions often retain substantial target alignment. Effect is strongly associated with signed cosine within the sign-randomized family ($ρ=.94$); $25.3\%$ of its draws exceed cosine $.5$, and every draw exceeding the observed mean effect has cosine above $.80$. This alignment leakage does not by itself invalidate a conditional randomization test; it limits what the comparator can distinguish and motivates reporting exchangeability assumptions, a construction diagnostic $A$, and the empirical cosine distribution. The primary Qwen complete gate remains negative because the protected tail fails all families. On independent data, continuous margin transfers only in Qwen and accuracy transfers in no selected cell. Prospectively registered language controls pass the complete gate in Qwen and DeepSeek, while a passing DeepSeek detox comparator rules out categorical separation; all nominal passes are sensitive to $Γ=1.10$. Frozen three-rater open-generation evaluation supports factual correction in DeepSeek but not Qwen; the automatic judge fails calibration (macro-F1 $.562$), so null-wide semantic results remain descriptive. SteerCheck makes these conditional and mixed conclusions auditable.

</details>


### [33] [FARCA: Fact-Aligned Reliability-Aware Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning with Factual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24350)
*Qiming Xie,Wenjie Zheng,Xiangqing Shen,Rui Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FARCA框架通过将事实监督转化为细粒度、可靠性加权的token级训练信号，解决大语言模型RLVR训练中由结果驱动奖励导致的噪声事实信用分配问题，在多个事实推理基准上显著提升模型事实性同时保持通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练时，结果驱动的奖励会引入幻觉风险。现有缓解方法引入过程级事实监督，但由于事实信号粒度聚合过粗且缺乏对信号可靠性的评估，导致事实验证与策略更新之间存在错配，作者将其定义为"噪声事实信用分配"，包含信用定位模糊和信用可靠性模糊两个维度。

Method: FARCA（Fact-Aligned Reliability-Aware Credit Assignment）包含两个核心创新：1) 细粒度信用定位：将事实验证的粒度与策略更新的粒度对齐，把事实监督转化为token级训练信号；2) 反事实证据归因：利用事实判断对关键证据的依赖性作为验证可靠性的经验代理，计算可靠性权重，用于调节事实奖励和局部策略优势，降低不可靠信号对策略优化的影响。

Result: 在不同模型和多个事实推理基准上的实验表明，FARCA显著提升了模型的事实性，同时保持了通用推理能力。

Conclusion: FARCA通过粒度对齐与可靠性加权的信用分配机制，有效缓解了RLVR训练中的事实幻觉问题，为如何将事实监督更精确地注入策略优化提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: To reduce the hallucination risk caused by outcome-driven rewards in large language models trained through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, existing mitigation approaches introduce process-level factual supervision. However, due to coarse-grained aggregation of factual signals and the lack of reliability assessment for these signals, they create a mismatch between fact verification and policy updates. We term this noisy factual credit assignment and decompose it into two aspects: credit localization ambiguity and credit reliability ambiguity. To address these issues, we propose FARCA (Fact-Aligned Reliability-Aware Credit Assignment), a policy optimization framework that transforms factual supervision into localized, reliability-weighted token-level training signals. FARCA achieves fine-grained credit localization by aligning the granularity of fact verification with that of policy updates. It further introduces counterfactual evidence attribution, which uses the dependence of a factual judgment on key evidence as an empirical proxy for verification reliability to compute reliability weights. These weights modulate factual rewards and local policy advantages, reducing the influence of potentially unreliable signals on policy optimization. Experiments across different models and multiple factual reasoning benchmarks show that FARCA significantly improves model factuality while preserving general reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [34] [Words, Spaces and Generative AI: Layers of language in contemporary architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24360)
*Anca-Simona Horvath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本章将语言视为建筑设计材料，探讨在文本到X生成式AI工具日益普及的背景下，语言在建筑设计中扮演的关键角色。文章从维特根斯坦到乔姆斯基、莱考夫及舍恩的概念隐喻理论出发，提出建筑生成式AI设计中交织着三种语言形式：话语（自然语言）、编程语言和注释，并勾勒了将生成隐喻与生成式AI连接的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着文本到X生成式AI模型成为建筑实践中的常见工具，语言作为设计材料的角色变得前所未有地重要。作者认为需要从语言学视角重新审视语言在AI辅助建筑设计中的功能与潜力，以填补当前研究中对语言维度关注的缺失。

Method: 通过梳理语言学发展脉络（从维特根斯坦、乔姆斯基到莱考夫，以及舍恩提出的概念隐喻和生成隐喻），将其与当代建筑设计和生成式AI工具进行理论连接，提出一个包含话语、编程语言和注释三种语言形式交织的分析框架，并据此提出三方面研究议程。

Result: 提出建筑生成式AI设计中三种语言形式的交织框架，并给出研究议程：(a)对建筑文本进行语料库语言学研究（结合主题建模等定量工具与话语分析等定性工具）；(b)将传播理论与信息研究引入建筑学研究；(c)意识到不同自然语言具有不同可供性，生成隐喻和概念隐喻会因语言而异。

Conclusion: 语言在生成式AI辅助的建筑设计中是核心设计材料，需要跨学科（语言学、传播学、信息科学）的研究方法来深入理解生成隐喻与生成式AI之间的关系，同时必须考虑多语言差异对设计隐喻的影响。

Abstract: Language can be considered a design material in architecture, and in the context of text-to-X generative AI models becoming a common tool for architectural practice, looking more closely at language is more important now than in the past. After describing some of the important developments in linguistics starting from Wittgenstein, and including the work of Chomsky, Lakoff, conceptual and generative metaphors as proposed by Schön, this chapter connects them to contemporary architectural design and generative text-to-X tools. The chapter builds on the idea that three main forms of language intertwine in architectural design done using generative AI, namely (I) discourse (or natural language which can contain professional terminology specific to our field), (II) programming languages (which are artificial languages sitting at the basis of all computational systems), and (III) annotations (as language elements attached to pieces of data). It concludes by outlining a research agenda for connecting generative metaphors to generative AI: (a) conducting corpus linguistics studies on architectural texts (using quantitative tools such as topic modelling, and qualitative tools such as discourse analysis); (b) bringing communication theory and information studies closer to architectural research and (c) taking into account that different (natural) languages come with different affordances meaning generative and conceptual metaphors differ in relation to this.

</details>


### [35] [Shortcut Before Circuit: Document Statistics Time In-Context Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24460)
*Yijun Liao,Fanwei Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明在自然数据中，当多个线索（新近性、重复性、位置）共同预测一个事实时，模型可能依赖其中任意一个，但数据本身无法区分。通过构建合成语言，作者将"新近性"与"稀有性"设为恰好共延（coextensive），再通过最小因果编辑将其分离，发现所有75次训练在干预下都无法复现逐单元（per-cell）读出，说明共同延展的规则使优化目标在机制间无差异，机制归因（mechanistic attribution）在此情况下不可得。然而，时序（timing）是稳定的：模型逃离位置捷径的时间可被闭式表达式预测。因此，语料能固定机制"何时出现"，但无法固定"是哪一个"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据中，当一个事实由多条线索（如新近性、重复性、位置）同时支持时，这些线索通常不冲突，因此无法从行为上判断模型究竟依赖哪条线索。现有"机制归因"研究往往假定行为一致即可推断机制，但作者指出这一假定在共同延展线索下不成立，需要一个精确可控制的环境来检验：当线索被分离时，模型的内部机制是否可被唯一确定，以及何时归因在原则上是不可行的。

Method: 作者训练26M参数的Transformer，使用一种合成语言，其中"新近性"和"稀有性"被设计为完全共延。通过一个最小因果编辑将其中一个线索反转，同时保持真值、token数量、答案位置不变，从而在行为和计算上均无法区分的条件下，将两种可能机制（依赖新近性 vs. 依赖稀有性）分离。作者在75个种子下重复训练，使用逐单元读出（per-cell readout）的符号分数（sign fraction）衡量干预前后的机制一致性，并分析逃离位置捷径的时间时序。

Result: 所有75次运行准确率>=0.999（即使在平凡启发式失效时），说明单一语料无法从行为上区分机制。干预下，25个聚类细胞中有13个的符号分数在三个种子间差异>0.3，最大差异达0.879（标准误为0.025），表明机制不可复现。方差大小与优化释放量相关。可复现的是时序：逃离位置捷径存在闭式上界，且随冗余度单调变化。在对该捷径探测之前，75次运行中有32次在准确率不变的情况下归因符号反转，说明门控于电路形成上是必要但不充分的。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：语料只能决定一个机制"何时（when）"出现（即可预测的时间线），而不能决定"是哪一个（which one）"（即机制身份）。共同延展的规则使优化目标在候选机制之间无差异，因此在这种情况下，对数据的机制归因在原则上不可用（unavailable），且作者通过精确构造使这种不可用情况成为精确可证的事实。这为"何时可以做机制归因"提供了一个清晰判据。

Abstract: When a context asserts two values for one fact, a model commits to a cue -- recency, repetition, position -- but natural data rarely makes these disagree, so behavior cannot reveal which. We train 26M-parameter transformers on a synthetic language where recency and rarity are exactly coextensive, and separate them with a minimal causal edit that inverts one cue while holding the truth, token count and answer position fixed. All 75 runs reach accuracy >= 0.999, including where the trivial heuristic fails, so no held-in evaluation distinguishes them. Under intervention the per-cell readout does not replicate: 13 of 25 cells differ by more than 0.3 in sign fraction across three seeds, the largest by 0.879 against a standard error of 0.025. The construction predicts this -- coextensive rules leave the objective indifferent between them -- and the variance is ordered by how much of the optimization each comparison releases. What replicates is timing: escape from a positional shortcut with a closed-form ceiling, monotone in redundancy. Probed before that escape, attribution reverses sign in 32 of 75 runs at unchanged accuracy, and gating on circuit formation is necessary but not sufficient. The corpus fixes when a mechanism appears, not which one -- a criterion for when mechanistic attribution to data is available at all, and our construction makes the unavailable case exact.

</details>

*Ana Gjorgjevikj,Barbara Koroušić Seljak,Tome Eftimov*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文本嵌入模型支持跨语言知识迁移，但其评估在高低资源语言之间极不均匀。现有评估方法在数据集稀缺的背景下缺乏系统性的可靠性分析框架，导致基准结论的可信度难以量化。

Method: 提出一个两维分析框架，区分任务特定评估与跨任务评估，并联合分析三个互补方面：(1)排名鲁棒性——在不同排名方法和数据组成下的稳定性；(2)模型一致性——跨任务泛化能力；(3)证据强度——引入Evidence Strength Score量化数据集可用性、多样性和鲁棒性可评估性。

Result: 分析揭示了严重的基准稀疏性，许多斯拉夫语言-任务对依赖单一数据集或高度相关的基准集合。跨任务分析发现一小群高迁移性模型（llama-embed-nemotron-8b、multilingual-e5-large-instruct、Qwen3-Embedding变体）在斯拉夫语言和任务上表现一致优异。

Conclusion: 基准排名和鲁棒性结论必须与证据强度联合解读，基准稀缺是多语言可靠评估的主要障碍；排名方法的变化和数据组成会影响模型排序的稳定性。

Abstract: Multilingual text embedding models enable cross-lingual transfer of knowledge across a wide range of NLP tasks, but their evaluation remains highly uneven across high-, mid- and low-resource languages. In this paper, we propose a two-dimensional framework, specifically tailored for analyzing multilingual embedding benchmarks under dataset scarcity, and apply it on the Slavic-language subset of the MTEB benchmark. The framework distinguishes between task-specific and cross-task evaluation, while jointly analyzing three complementary aspects: (1) ranking robustness, (2) model consistency, and (3) evidence strength. At the task-specific level, we evaluate the stability of model rankings under changes in ranking methodology and benchmark dataset composition. At the cross-task level, we assess the ability of models to generalize across diverse tasks within a language. To quantify the reliability of benchmark conclusions, we introduce an Evidence Strength Score that accounts for dataset availability, diversity, and robustness assessability. Our analysis reveals severe benchmark sparsity, with many Slavic language-task pairs relying on a single dataset or highly correlated benchmark collections, limiting the ability to draw robust conclusions. The cross-task analysis reveals a small group of highly transferable models, most notably llama-embed-nemotron-8b, multilingual-e5-large-instruct, and Qwen3-Embedding variants, that consistently perform well across Slavic languages and tasks. Overall, the results demonstrate that benchmark rankings and robustness conclusions must be interpreted jointly with certain notation of their evidence strength and highlight benchmark scarcity as a major obstacle to trustworthy multilingual evaluation.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESR（Expected-Severity-Risk），一种后果感知的问题监督目标，用于主动医疗对话中的信息获取。ESR不仅考虑新证据的信息量，还考虑其对下游诊断决策（尤其是高严重度错误）的影响，通过边际化可能的答案并使用前缀语言策略进行蒸馏，在DDxPlus数据集上显著降低了高严重度诊断漏诊率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息寻求方法主要优先选择能最大程度降低诊断不确定性的问题，但这一标准忽略了医疗诊断的重要特性：不同类型的诊断错误后果差异巨大。漏掉严重疾病可能比在后果较轻的选项间降低不确定性更为重要。因此，问题获取不仅要考虑新证据的信息丰富度，还应考虑其对下游诊断决策的预期影响。

Method: 提出Expected-Severity-Risk（ESR）目标，每个候选问题按其预期降低的严重度感知终末风险进行评分。由于问题在观察答案之前必须被选择，ESR使用训练集仅有的总体统计数据对可能的答案进行边际化。然后将ESR的排名蒸馏到前缀仅语言策略中，使部署时的下一问题选择不需要教师侧计算。

Result: 在DDxPlus数据集上、使用三个Qwen3-4B训练种子，匹配ESR监督将平均高严重度诊断漏诊从0.0645降至0.0455（降低29.5%），平均诊断准确率从0.9123提升至0.9320，且每个对话仅需额外0.14个问题。固定预算分析表明在控制问题数量时两个目标行为仍然不同，而匹配的期望0/1风险控制表明严重度感知加权在通用决策感知监督之上改善了高严重度错误剖面。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将主动医疗对话从单纯的不确定性降低转向后果感知的证据获取，表明在医疗对话系统中考虑诊断错误的后果差异性是更有价值的方向。

Abstract: Proactive medical dialogue requires an agent to decide what to ask from incomplete patient information. Existing information-seeking approaches commonly prioritize questions that most reduce diagnostic uncertainty. While effective for acquiring informative evidence, this criterion overlooks an important property of medical diagnosis: different diagnostic errors can carry substantially different consequences. Missing a severe condition may matter more than reducing uncertainty among less consequential alternatives. Question acquisition should therefore consider not only how informative new evidence is, but also how it is expected to affect the downstream diagnostic decision. To this end, we propose Expected-Severity-Risk (ESR), a consequence-aware question-supervision objective that values each candidate by its expected reduction in severity-aware terminal risk. Because questions must be selected before their answers are observed, ESR marginalizes over possible answers using train-only population statistics. Its rankings are then distilled into a prefix-only language policy, so next-question selection requires no teacher-side computation at deployment. Across three Qwen3-4B training seeds on DDxPlus, matched ESR supervision reduces mean high-severity diagnostic miss from .0645 to .0455 (-29.5%) and improves mean diagnostic accuracy from .9123 to .9320 while requiring only 0.14 additional questions per dialogue. Fixed-budget analyses show that the two objectives remain behaviorally distinct when question count is controlled, while a matched expected-0/1-risk control shows that severity-aware weighting improves the high-severity error profile beyond generic decision-aware supervision. These results support moving proactive medical dialogue beyond uncertainty reduction toward consequence-aware evidence acquisition.

</details>


### [38] [Is Discrete Difficulty Sufficient? Leveraging Continuous Difficulty for Efficient Self-Consistency in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24590)
*Sihyeong Yeom,Geon Park,Geunyeong Jeong,Taewoong Yoon,Jaewook Lee,Harksoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Flexible Self-Consistency (FSC)方法，通过预训练探针预测输出熵作为连续难度信号，动态调整推理路径数量，在保持与SC相当准确率的同时，节省高达76%的token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-Consistency (SC)解码策略虽然能提升复杂推理任务的性能，但生成多条推理路径会消耗大量token，计算效率低下。现有优化方法将问题难度划分为少量固定等级，无法充分捕捉推理复杂度的连续变化特性，因此需要一种能够连续估计难度并动态分配采样预算的方法。

Method: FSC方法使用预训练的探针(probe)预测输入问题的输出熵，将熵作为模型不确定性的连续指标，据此灵活控制推理路径的生成数量。问题越不确定（熵越高），生成更多推理路径；问题越确定（熵越低），生成更少推理路径，从而实现采样预算的动态分配。

Result: 在多种模型和基准测试上，FSC与标准SC相比在保持相当准确率的同时，最多可节省76%的token消耗。

Conclusion: FSC通过连续难度估计替代固定难度分级，有效解决了SC的计算效率瓶颈，在准确率几乎不损失的情况下大幅节省token，为自一致性解码策略提供了更高效的灵活变体。

Abstract: Self-Consistency (SC) is a decoding strategy that samples diverse reasoning paths and selects the most consistent answer, demonstrating strong performance on complex reasoning problems. However, the excessive token consumption incurred by generating multiple reasoning paths has been identified as a major limitation of SC. To improve computational efficiency, several studies have proposed strategies that adjust the number of reasoning paths or allocate resources differentially according to problem difficulty. Nevertheless, most existing methods categorize difficulty into a few fixed levels, failing to fully capture the continuously varying nature of reasoning complexity. In this work, we propose Flexible Self-Consistency (FSC), which estimates problem difficulty as a continuous signal and dynamically adjusts the number of generated reasoning paths accordingly. FSC predicts the output entropy of an input question using a pre-trained probe and leverages it as an indicator of model uncertainty to flexibly control the sampling budget. Experimental results show that, across various models and benchmarks, FSC maintains accuracy comparable to SC while achieving token savings of up to 76%.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了后训练量化对孟加拉语（Bangla）自然语言理解任务的影响，发现不同模型族对量化格式的响应差异显著：GPT-OSS在推理密集型任务下GPTQ格式下损失高达57.35%的准确率，而Qwen和LLaMA在GPTQ下保持稳定甚至偶尔超过全精度模型；布尔问答类理解任务则对所有模型和格式都保持稳定。研究结论指出，量化对孟加拉语部署可行，但架构和量化方法的选择比位宽本身更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前关于大语言模型后训练量化效果的知识几乎完全来自英语基准测试，而在形态复杂、资源匮乏的语言（如孟加拉语）上是否同样成立尚不明确。这一研究空白可能导致低资源语言场景下的部署决策缺乏依据，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 评估三个模型族（Qwen-2.5-7B、LLaMA-3.1-8B、GPT-OSS-20B），在全精度和三种量化格式（GPTQ-Int8、GPTQ-Q8、GGUF-W8A16）下，通过lm-evaluation-harness对五个孟加拉语NLU基准（Bangla MMLU、CommonsenseQA-BN、OpenBookQA-BN、PIQA-BN、BoolQ-BN）进行零样本评估。

Result: GPT-OSS在GGUF-W8A16格式下推理密集型任务损失最高达57.35%准确率；Qwen和LLaMA在GPTQ下保持稳定，少数情况下量化版本甚至优于全精度版本；BoolQ-BN作为理解类任务在所有三个模型族和所有格式下均保持稳定。三个模型族对量化的响应方式各不相同。

Conclusion: 量化可以很好地应用于孟加拉语部署，但对于在受限硬件上运行模型的从业者而言，选择模型架构和量化方法比单纯关注位宽更为关键。

Abstract: Post-training quantization lowers the memory footprint of Large Language Models (LLMs) and speeds up inference, which is why it is now common for on-device deployment. Most of what we know about its effects, however, comes from English benchmarks. It is not clear whether the same holds for morphologically complex, low-resource languages such as Bangla, and this gap is what we address here. We evaluate three model families---Qwen-2.5-7B, LLaMA-3.1-8B, and GPT-OSS-20B---in full precision and in three quantized formats (GPTQ-Int8, GPTQ-Q8, GGUF-W8A16) across five Bangla natural language understanding benchmarks (Bangla MMLU, CommonsenseQA-BN, OpenBookQA-BN, PIQA-BN, and BoolQ-BN), using zero-shot evaluation through lm-evaluation-harness. To our knowledge this is the first controlled comparison of quantization formats on Bangla NLU. The three families do not respond the same way: GPT-OSS loses up to 57.35% accuracy on reasoning-heavy tasks under GGUF-W8A16, while Qwen and LLaMA hold steady under GPTQ, and in a few cases the quantized version edges out the full-precision one. BoolQ-BN, a comprehension task, stays stable across all three families regardless of format. Taken together, these results suggest quantization can work well for Bangla deployment, but the choice of architecture and quantization method matters more than the bit width alone. We discuss what this means for practitioners choosing a model to run on constrained hardware.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Semantic Accuracy: Consequence-Aware Evaluation for Safety-Critical Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24621)
*Yujing Chang,Thinh Pham,Van-Phat Thai,Chunyao Ma,Yash Guleria,Pham Nhut Huy,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在航空交通管制（ATC）等安全关键场景中的可靠性问题，发现传统语义指标（如F1）会高估模型的实际操作可靠性。作者提出后果感知评估框架，结合40名空中交通管制员的反馈构建诊断性基准，对8个模型进行评估，揭示了系统性"语义-安全差距"（semantic-safety gap）。即使风险感知微调能缩小这一差距，也无法消除，表明后果感知评估是安全关键部署前必要的补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键操作中，模型的强语义性能并不能保证操作可靠性。标准NLP指标（如F1）无法捕捉错误后果的不对称性——例如误读高度、遗漏执行条件或混淆呼号，即使这些错误在语义指标上得分尚可，其操作后果却可能截然不同且严重。作者通过航空交通管制这一高容错需求领域，质疑语义分数是否真实反映操作可靠性。

Method: 提出后果感知评估（consequence-aware evaluation）框架，基于航空标准和三个国家40名ATC管制员的反馈构建受控诊断性ATC基准。在该基准上对8个语言模型进行评估，比较传统语义指标与后果感知评估的结果差距，并进一步验证风险感知微调对缩小该差距的作用。

Result: 研究发现存在系统性的语义-安全差距：传统评分对模型性能的估计显著高于后果感知评估的结果，即使是在标准指标下看似可靠的模型也如此。风险感知微调能够缩小但不能消除这一差距，说明仅靠微调无法完全弥合语义指标与操作可靠性之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 在任何真实安全关键部署声称之前，后果感知评估是标准NLP语义指标的必要补充。语义指标的高分不能作为安全关键操作中模型可靠性的充分证据，需要引入能反映错误不对称后果的评估机制。

Abstract: Can language models be trusted in safety- critical operations? In such settings, strong per- formance on semantic metrics does not guaran- tee operational reliability: a misread altitude, a dropped execution condition, or a confused call- sign may score well under standard F1 yet carry sharply asymmetric operational consequences. We study this problem in air traffic control (ATC), where controller-pilot communication demands near-zero error tolerance, and use consequence-aware evaluation to test whether semantic scores misstate operational reliabil- ity. The framework is instantiated in a con- trolled diagnostic ATC benchmark grounded in aviation standards and feedback from 40 air traffic controllers across three countries. Evaluating 8 models, we uncover a system- atic semantic-safety gap: conventional scores give substantially higher performance estimates than consequence-aware evaluation, even for models that appear reliable under standard met- rics. Risk-aware fine-tuning narrows but does not close this gap, showing that consequence- aware evaluation is a necessary complement to standard NLP metrics before any real safety- critical deployment claim

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话式AI用户感知研究多将用户态度视为静态快照，忽略了模型发布、功能更新、安全干预和访问策略变化等干预事件对用户感知的动态影响，缺乏长期、大规模的系统性分析。

Method: 对Reddit讨论进行长期大规模分析，采用情感分类（sentiment classification）与主题概念分析（thematic concept analysis）相结合的方法，系统考察不同AI提供商（Anthropic、OpenAI、Grok、DeepSeek）的模型发布干预如何影响用户感知。

Result: 研究发现CAIS用户感知是动态且对干预敏感的：Anthropic通过Claude Code和产品-模型匹配呈现最清晰的正向发布画像；OpenAI在GPT-5和GPT-5.1发布后呈现反弹和恢复的动态模式；Grok-3的感知受提供者身份和政治话语塑造；DeepSeek-R1则兼具工程赞誉与对审查、可访问性及可靠性的担忧。

Conclusion: 模型发布不仅仅是技术更新，而是面向用户的干预行为，会重塑用户的情感态度、期望和公共讨论。研究为理解AI系统演变过程中用户感知的动态性提供了重要实证依据。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems (CAISes) continuously change through model releases, feature updates, safety interventions, and access-policy shifts, yet user perceptions are often studied as static snapshots. We conduct a long-term, large-scale analysis of Reddit discussions to examine how users perceive CAIS model release interventions across providers. By combining sentiment classification and thematic concept analysis, we show that CAIS perceptions are dynamic and intervention-sensitive. Anthropic exhibits the clearest positive release profile through Claude Code and product-model fit, OpenAI shows backlash-and-recovery dynamics around GPT-5 and GPT-5.1, Grok-3 is shaped by provider identity and political discourse, and DeepSeek-R1 combines engineering praise with concerns about censorship, access, and reliability. These findings show that model releases are not merely technical updates, but user-facing interventions that reshape sentiment, expectations, and public discussion.

</details>


### [42] [From local kernels to global form: modeling the emergence of musical content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24660)
*Francesco Vitucci,Michele Lorusso,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于滑动窗口的观察驱动估计机制，用于从单一符号序列推导局部转移核轨迹，而非依赖外部形式划分。在德彪西《Syrinx》的273个音符事件上测试该机制，验证其与A-B-A'结构参考边界的对齐情况。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马尔可夫模型已被广泛用于符号音乐建模，包括非齐次形式。但现有的非齐次方法通常依赖外生的、预先给定的形式分段（如乐句划分），缺乏一种纯粹由数据观察驱动的局部核估计方式。

Method: 采用重叠滑动窗口从单一符号序列中生成局部转移核的轨迹，无需外生形式划分。在德彪西《Syrinx》的273个逻辑音符事件上验证，采用A-B-A'读法作为参考而非黄金标准；对绝对音高核和记谱时值核分别应用相同的验证，并测量Jensen-Shannon距离。

Result: 在L=6时，两个参考边界在音高与时值两个维度上均达到Jensen-Shannon最大值；时值平台显著更窄（64/267）而音高平台很宽（210/267）。滑动窗口比较的理论最大值为1/√(L-1)，因此L=6时音高值及宽平台本身不足以作为强证据。两者跨维度对齐与边界敏感性一致，但宽平台排除了将其作为唯一自动分段器的可能。500次重合成实验量化了两维度相对源的偏离，并在L=2时发现精确复制退化现象。

Conclusion: 观察驱动的滑动窗口局部核估计在捕捉结构边界方面具有跨维度一致性，但宽平台表明该方法不应单独作为自动分段器使用；需结合其他线索或进一步约束才能有效利用。

Abstract: Markov models are established tools for symbolic music, including non-homogeneous formulations. The narrower contribution examined here is an observation-driven estimation mechanism: overlapping sliding windows derive a trajectory of local transition kernels from one symbolic sequence rather than from an exogenous formal partition. We test this mechanism on 273 logical note events from Debussy's Syrinx (1913), using the often-proposed A-B-A' reading as a reference rather than ground truth. We apply the same validation to absolute-pitch and notated-duration kernels. At $L=6$, both reference boundaries attain the Jensen--Shannon maximum in both dimensions; the duration plateau is substantially narrower (64 of 267 comparisons) than the pitch plateau (210 of 267). Because the theoretical maximum for consecutive sliding-window comparisons is set by window geometry and equals $1/\sqrt{L-1}$ for maximal turnover of the entering/leaving transition, the pitch value at $L=6$ and its broad plateau are not, by themselves, strong evidence. Their cross-dimensional alignment is consistent with boundary sensitivity, while the broad plateaus preclude treating either curve alone as a unique automatic segmenter. Five-hundred-draw re-synthesis experiments quantify departure from the source in both dimensions and expose an exact-copy degeneracy at $L=2$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算法作曲中，Csound分数、雕刻记谱、实时控制和排练点击通常由不同模块独立生成，导致各自的时间线漂移（timeline drift），缺乏统一的同步机制。作者希望通过单一数据源编译多种后端输出，从根本上解决同步问题。

Method: 提出了一个分层的架构：时间层（rational beat timeline上的类型化实体存储）、语义层、渲染契约层。秒、采样率或赫兹的转换仅在渲染时发生；Csound音符使用外部.orc文件中的稳定命名乐器（named instruments），曲线以k-rate信号声明在score p-fields中；点击后端从同一节拍和速度推导排练音频，并复用Csound序列化器。最终编译为一套后端特定的契约生成各类型输出。

Result: 系统成功生成了Csound合成、Beta MusicXML 4.0、OSC控制和排练点击等多种输出，且这些输出因共享同一实体存储而保持同步。存档补充材料展示了paclet发布前的报告输出，并讨论了该系统在开源生态中作为专有创作环境的局限性。

Conclusion: 通过集中式不可变时间轴存储加后端契约编译的方式，可以可靠地保证多种创作输出（合成、记谱、实时控制、排练）保持时间同步，但也需权衡该系统作为专有环境与开源生态集成时的实用限制。

Abstract: One algorithmic composition may require a Csound score, engraved notation, real-time control, and a rehearsal click. Authored separately, their timelines drift. Temporal System is a Wolfram Language paclet that instead compiles one immutable store of typed entities on a rational beat timeline through backend-specific contracts. It emits Csound synthesis, beta MusicXML 4.0, OSC control, and click artifacts that remain synchronized because they share that store. Conversion to seconds, samples, or hertz occurs only at render time. Csound notes use stable named instruments in external .orc files; curves become k-rate signals declared against score p-fields. The click backend derives rehearsal audio from the same meter and tempo and reuses the Csound serializer. We describe the temporal, semantic, and rendering-contract layers, their practical trade-offs, and the limits of this proprietary authoring environment within an otherwise open-source ecosystem. The archived supplement exposes the reported outputs pending paclet release.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "低资源语言"这一笼统标签将多种不同的资源短缺维度混为一谈，导致对波斯语NLP资源状况的判断过于简化甚至误导。作者希望纠正这一认知，主张将"注释稀缺"视为该语言NLP资源生态的性质而非语言本身的固有属性，从而更精确地指导资源建设方向。

Method: 论文综合评述了截至2026年7月可获得的34个代表性波斯语文本资源，并附加三项定量交叉验证：(1) 基于W3Techs和Common Crawl的独立Web测量评估波斯语在网页内容语言中的可见度；(2) 对语音语料从FARSDAT到包含数百至数千小时语音的近期语料进行选择性梳理；(3) 以Common Crawl相对Web存在度为归一化基准，进行波斯语与英语任务特定标注量的匹配对比。

Result: W3Techs显示波斯语约占已知内容语言网站比例的0.9%，Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30中波斯语占HTML页面的0.7039%，位居前二十大内容语言之列。匹配对比显示波斯语句法和新闻命名实体识别标注相对密集，但自然语言推理标注量低于Web比例基线。不同任务间标注密度差异显著，不支持"波斯语整体缺乏标注量"的简单论断。

Conclusion: 波斯语的资源瓶颈不在于绝对的标注总量，而在于注释稀缺以非均匀方式分布：任务和领域覆盖不均衡、标注方案不兼容、访问与文档摩擦大，以及对专业领域、偏好数据和非标准伊朗波斯语变体的监督不足。因此应针对这些具体短板而非笼统地补充"更多资源"来改进。

Abstract: Persian (Farsi) is often described as a low-resource language in natural language processing, but that label collapses distinct shortages into a single category. This paper argues that Persian is more precisely described as annotation-scarce, provided that the term is understood as a property of its NLP resource ecology rather than an intrinsic property of the language. The review covers 34 representative Persian text resources available by July 2026 and adds three quantitative cross-checks. First, independent web measurements place Persian among roughly the twenty most visible content languages: W3Techs reports Persian on about 0.9% of websites with a known content language, while Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30 identifies Persian as the primary language of 0.7039% of HTML pages. Second, a selective speech review shows a long resource trajectory from FARSDAT to recent corpora containing hundreds or thousands of hours of speech. Third, a matched Persian-English comparison normalizes task-specific annotation volumes by relative Common Crawl web presence. The resulting ratios vary sharply: Persian syntax and news NER are comparatively dense, whereas natural-language inference falls below the web-proportional baseline. The evidence therefore does not support a simple claim that Persian is globally deficient in labeled volume. Instead, annotation scarcity is expressed through uneven task and domain coverage, incompatible schemes, access and documentation friction, and limited supervision for specialist domains, preference data, and varieties beyond standard Iranian Persian.

</details>

Abstract: While text-based hallucination detection has been extensively studied, spoken hallucination detection remains largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. We present the first multilingual spoken hallucination benchmark comprising 12,013 news samples across English, Russian, and Kazakh with controlled hallucinations of three types and three severity levels. Samples comprise original articles and aligned hallucinated counterparts in text and audio. We complement the synthetic corpus with 290 fact-checked fake news items collected natively in Russian (225) and Kazakh (65), translated into the other language and rendered through the same TTS-ASR pipeline. We assess fine-tuned multilingual encoders and, in zero-shot in-context settings, multimodal decoder models on transcript-based versus direct audio processing. Transcript-based detection generally outperforms direct audio processing, with binary-task degradation for strong encoders tracking per-language ASR error. On real-world fakes, synthetic-trained detectors transfer strongly (macro-F1 0.82-0.88 on original text), while Russian provenance analysis reveals both veracity-related and model-dependent machine-style signals, quantifying a key confound in synthetic hallucination benchmarks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的多边形合成使用恒定角速度，仅能处理规则参数化定义的多边形，且波形仅在单一形状族内平滑过渡。现有方法无法处理任意顶点配置（如不规则或星形多边形），也无法在不同顶点数的多边形之间进行平滑变形，更未扩展到三维空间。这限制了多边形合成作为通用波形生成工具的灵活性与表达力。

Method: 本文提出三项核心扩展：(1) 将振荡器适配为从外部缓冲区接受任意顶点配置，使所有封闭多边形（规则、不规则或星形）均可作为波形生成器；(2) 设计混合插值算法，使顶点数不同的多边形之间能够平滑变形，通过中间形状桥接不同拓扑； (3) 将范式扩展至三维：凸多面体绕三轴旋转后由固定水平平面切片，截面产生由固体方位连续控制的可变多边形。系统在RNBO中实现，采用几何缓存策略避免逐样本重算。抗混叠结合四点多边形BLAMP校正（基于运行时Bézier切线推导）与自适应过采样，以适应一般顶点配置而无需逐形状解析推导。

Result: 该系统在RNBO（Cycling '74）中成功运行，通过几何缓存策略实现高效计算（无需每样本重算），并实现了对任意顶点配置通用的抗混叠方案——利用运行时Bézier切线推导的四点polyBLAMP校正配合自适应过采样，避免了传统方案需要逐形状解析推导的局限。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一弧长引擎实现了多边形合成的通用化：任意闭环顶点数据均可作为波形来源，支持跨拓扑平滑变形，并突破了二维限制进入三维形状控制领域，为音频合成提供了更加广泛且灵活的波形设计空间。

Abstract: Polygonal synthesis generates audio by traversing the perimeter of a polygon with a phasor; prior work uses a constant angular velocity, whereas the proposed system adopts constant arc-length (perimeter) velocity. Existing formulations operate on regular, parametrically defined polygons, producing smooth timbral transitions within a single family of shapes.
  This paper generalizes polygonal synthesis around a unified arc-length engine: vertex data of any origin feed the same DSP pipeline. First, we adapt the oscillator to accept arbitrary vertex configurations from an external buffer, opening the possibility for a broad class of closed polygons -- regular, irregular, or star-shaped -- to function as a waveform generator. Second, a hybrid interpolation algorithm enables smooth morphing between polygons with unequal vertex counts, passing through intermediate shapes that have no parametric description. Third, we extend the paradigm to three dimensions: a convex polyhedron rotated about three axes is sliced by a fixed horizontal plane, and the resulting cross-section yields a continuously variable polygon controlled by the solid's orientation. The system runs in RNBO (Cycling~'74) with a geometry caching strategy that avoids per-sample recomputation. Antialiasing combines a four-point polyBLAMP correction derived from runtime Bézier tangents with adaptive oversampling, adapting the correction geometrically to general vertex configurations without per-shape analytical derivation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练的LLM智能体大多是回合制的，无法跨回合积累可复用知识。虽然SkillRL等技能学习方法尝试从轨迹中提取技能，但技能库被视为只能追加的仓库，无法验证已存储技能是否仍然有效。

Method: SkillForge通过三个关键设计来实现持续的技能演化：1) 在智能体交互过程中显式化技能使用，使RL能同时优化环境动作和技能调用决策；2) 引入基于证据的技能验证机制；3) 提出多路径技能归纳方法，使技能库在持续增长的同时维持质量。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld三个基准上的大量实验表明，SkillForge在性能上持续优于SkillRL，验证了持续验证的技能在训练更强LLM智能体方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 持续验证和精炼技能是提升LLM智能体长期学习能力的关键，SkillForge证明了动态技能库优于静态追加式技能库。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for complex decision-making tasks. However, most RL-trained agents remain episodic and cannot accumulate reusable knowledge across episodes. Recent skill-based approaches, such as SkillRL, attempt to address this issue by extracting skills from raw trajectories, but treat the skill bank as an append-only repository without verifying whether stored skills remain effective. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a framework for continuous skill evolution that enables skills to be verified and refined through environment interaction. By making skill usage explicit during agent interaction, RL can directly optimize both environment actions and skill invocation decisions. SkillForge further introduces evidence-based skill verification and multi-pathway skill induction, allowing the skill bank to continuously grow while maintaining its quality. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld show that SkillForge consistently outperforms SkillRL, demonstrating the effectiveness of continuously verified skills in training stronger LLM agents.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯评估框架，用于联合建模RAG系统中的检索成功、弃答行为和答案正确性，通过条件分解揭示系统组件间的交互与错误传播，可结合人类标注和LLM-as-a-judge标注进行统一概率建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统评估不仅需要端到端正确性，还需理解各组件如何交互以及错误如何在管线中传播。传统边际指标无法揭示行为差异，需要更细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯评估框架，根据管线信息流对检索成功、弃答行为和答案正确性进行联合分解建模；区分任务成功（用户获得正确答案）与生成器行为适当性；扩展模型将LLM-as-a-judge标注作为校准的噪声观测纳入。

Result: 在27种RAG配置（3个数据集×3个检索器×3个生成器）上的实验表明，条件分解能揭示在边际指标下表现等价但行为差异显著的系统；检索成功标注比任务成功标注对策略遵从估计更具信息量，并通过信息论解释该不对称性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为RAG系统的细粒度评估提供了统一的概率建模方案，支持将有限的人类标注与廉价的自动化评估结合，有助于更好地理解和改进RAG管线中各组件的协同行为。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires assessing not only end-to-end correctness but also how individual components interact and how errors propagate through the pipeline. We introduce a Bayesian evaluation framework that jointly models retrieval success, abstention behavior, and answer correctness, factorized according to the pipeline's information flow. The model distinguishes task success. Whether the user received a correct answer (from generator success) and whether the generator behaved appropriately given the retrieval outcome. We apply the framework to 27 RAG configurations across three datasets, three retrievers, and three generators, and show that the conditional decomposition reveals substantial behavioral differences between systems that appear equivalent under marginal metrics. We further analyze the annotation allocation problem, demonstrating that retrieval-success annotations are more informative than task-success annotations for estimating policy adherence, and provide an information-theoretic explanation for this asymmetry. Finally, we extend the model to incorporate LLM-as-a-judge annotations as calibrated noisy observations, enabling practitioners to combine limited human judgments with cheaper automated assessments within a unified probabilistic model.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD方法忽略了真实描述中空间约束缺失的挑战，导致生成的CAD程序不满足有效的构造要求。作者认为缺失的空间约束应基于底层构造结构和可复用的设计经验进行推断。

Method: ExpConCAD框架包含三个步骤：(1)恢复预期的构造结构和约束范围；(2)为相似范围检索相关的约束补全经验以完成缺失的空间约束；(3)生成可执行的CadQuery程序。

Result: 大量实验证明ExpConCAD的有效性，并揭示了构造结构理解和经验记忆在空间约束补全中的作用。

Conclusion: 空间约束缺失问题应通过结合构造结构理解与经验记忆来解决，这为文本到CAD任务提供了新思路；代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD aims to generate executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, real-world descriptions are often underspecified and omit critical spatial constraints required for valid CAD construction, a challenge that has been largely overlooked by existing methods. In this paper, we argue that missing spatial constraints should be inferred with respect to the underlying construction structure and informed by reusable design experience. Based on this insight, we propose ExpConCAD, an experience-enhanced framework for implicit spatial constraint completion. ExpConCAD first recovers the intended construction structure and constraint scopes, then retrieves relevant constraint-completion experience for similar scopes to complete the missing spatial constraints, and finally generates executable CadQuery programs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ExpConCAD and provide insights into the role of construction structure understanding and experience memory in spatial constraint completion. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hotjiashell/ExpConCAD.

</details>


### [50] [Linear Probing Provides Robust and Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24780)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Hanqi Yan,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandecic,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现机器生成文本（MGT）与人类书写文本（HWT）的潜在表示在低维空间中线性可分，并基于此提出简单线性探针（linear probes）作为MGT检测器。该探针在4个基准上优于16个基线，OOD检测AUC提升+11，仅需不到100个样本即可达到接近峰值性能，并能提取共享的潜在"MGT方向"用于跨域泛化及细粒度估计"机器性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测机器生成文本日益重要，但现有监督检测器在跨域（OOD）场景下性能退化严重，且需要大量多样化的训练数据。作者希望探索MGT与HWT在潜在表示空间中的本质差异，从而构建更稳健、样本效率更高的检测方法。

Method: 首先分析MGT与HWT潜在表示的线性可分性，并给出基于表示质量系统性差异的可解释性分析。基于此，训练两种简单线性探针（linear probes）变体，在4个基准上与16个基线对比评估，包括OOD检测性能和样本效率分析。通过探针向量揭示共享的潜在MGT方向，并利用其衡量连续"机器性"谱。

Result: 线性探针在4个基准上一致优于广泛基线，OOD检测较基线AUC提升+11；仅需少于100个训练样本即可达到接近峰值性能，显著优于需要大规模数据的监督检测器。探针的跨域迁移能力源于其恢复的共享潜在MGT方向，且该方向支持连续"机器性"的细粒度估计。

Conclusion: MGT与HWT在潜在空间中存在系统性的表示质量差异，导致线性可分性。简单线性探针是一种稳健、样本高效的MGT检测方案，兼具OOD泛化能力和对AI编辑文本的细粒度估计潜力，为MGT检测提供了新的理解和实用工具。

Abstract: Distinguishing machine-generated text (MGT) from human-written text (HWT) becomes increasingly important due to potential misuse. However, most supervised detectors often degrade out-of-domain (OOD) and require large, diverse training sets. In this work, we analyze the linearity and quality of MGT representations and show that simple linear probes outperform a wide range of detectors while being substantially more sample-efficient. We first show that MGT and HWT latent representations are linearly separable in low-dimensional space, and provide a plausible explanation for this separability through systematic differences in their representation quality. Motivated by these insights, we train two variants of simple linear probes and evaluate them across 4 benchmarks against 16 baselines. Probes consistently improve OOD detection (+11 AUC), requiring solely ${<}100$ samples to reach near-peak performance. We show that this transferability arises because probes recover a shared latent MGT direction that generalizes across diverse settings. Finally, we demonstrate that probing vectors capture a continuous spectrum of ``machineness'', highlighting their potential for fine-grained estimation of AI-edited text. Overall, our work provides insights into latent-space differences between MGT and HWT and demonstrates the potential of linear probes as as robust and sample-efficient MGT detectors. We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [51] [Structurally-bounded Agentic Graph Exploration for Evidence-Grounded Scholarly DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24809)
*Rima Hazra,Sayan Layek,Somnath Banerjee,Soumen Chakrabarti,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Crase，一种有界（bounded）且可检查（inspectable）的学术搜索替代方案，用于替代深度研究代理（deep research agents）。Crase不进行开放式搜索循环，而是仅查询一次搜索引擎获取种子论文，沿1.5跳引文邻域扩展，剪除缺乏蕴含支持（entailment support）的引文边，并用考虑新近度的随机游走对剩余论文排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究代理虽能力强大，但存在开放式搜索循环导致的候选集不可控、推理过程不可解释、停止条件不明确等问题，且依赖昂贵的专有模型。作者旨在提出一种固定所有推理阶段、成本更低且完全可检查的替代方案。

Method: Crase的四阶段流水线：(1) 对搜索引擎进行一次查询获取种子论文；(2) 沿1.5跳引文邻域扩展候选集；(3) 通过检测引文边的语义蕴含关系剪除缺乏支持的边；(4) 使用recency-aware随机游走排序剩余论文。所有阶段（候选集、保留理由、停止条件）在推理前即明确固定。

Result: 在LitSearch及另一个基准测试上，基于500K论文的arXiv语料库，Crase在recall@50上比基于专有模型的深度研究代理最高提升3倍，而成本约为其三分之一。

Conclusion: Crase证明了有界、可检查的搜索策略可以在学术搜索任务上超越无界但昂贵的深度研究代理，同时大幅降低成本，为构建更透明、可控的研究工具提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present Crase, a bounded and inspectable alternative to deep research agents for scholarly search. Instead of an open-ended search loop, Crase queries a search engine once for seed papers, expands them along their 1.5-hop citation neighborhood, prunes citation edges whose claims lack entailment support, and ranks the remaining papers with a recency-aware random walk. This makes the candidate set, the reason each paper is kept, and the stopping condition explicit and fixed before inference. On LitSearch and one further benchmarks over a 500K-paper arXiv corpus, Crase outperforms deep research agents built on proprietary models by up to 3$\times$ recall@50 at roughly a third of the cost.

</details>

*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [53] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BrowserForge是一个通过并行驱动大量浏览器沙箱在开放网络上生成Web代理交互数据的框架，生成了包含203,238条轨迹（每条来自不同网站）的大规模数据集，并经过清洗后用于微调多模态模型，使其在Online-Mind2Web上的成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于渲染像素（截屏）行动的Web代理面临训练数据稀缺的问题：公开数据集通常仅含数千条轨迹且局限于少量固定网站，现有自动化合成管线也被预定义的网站列表或教程来源所束缚，导致代理所见网站的多样性不足，无法支撑大规模高质量交互轨迹的生成。

Method: BrowserForge框架包含三个核心组件：（1）开放网络数据采集阶段，使代理接触数十万个真实可访问的网站；（2）沙箱集群管理器，调度数百个并发浏览器实现高利用率；（3）Proposer-Solver双智能体循环，将原始页面转化为可执行任务并收集经验证的轨迹。此外采用规则加模型的清洗管线过滤失败的运行，并将幸存的推理统一重写为链式思考（chain-of-thought）风格。页面结构（如可访问性树）仅作为合成阶段的信号，最终训练和发布的代理完全基于截屏行动。

Result: 生成的语料包含203,238条轨迹，每条来自不同的网站，规模和多样性均超过现有轨迹数据集。在该语料上微调紧凑型多模态模型，其在在线Online-Mind2Web上的成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%，并在静态Multimodal-Mind2Web上持续提升步骤准确率，且增益随语料规模增长而扩大。受控分析进一步证实开放网络数据采集和广泛的网站覆盖是性能提升的关键贡献因素。

Conclusion: BrowserForge证明通过驱动的并行浏览器沙箱在没有预定义网站列表约束的条件下，能够以大规模方式生成多样化、高质量的Web交互轨迹数据，为基于截屏的Web代理训练提供了一种可扩展的数据生产途径。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>

Abstract: We analyze the convergence rate of certain sequence of the duality gaps in the generalized Frank-Wolfe method, under certain regime of the weak growth condition proposed in Peña (2023). Our analysis leverages a recursion lemma that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [55] [Let AEDs Move: Urban Mobility Enhanced Defibrillator Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23810)
*Bahar Dehqani Viniche,Sheng Liu,Nooshin Salari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了平台驱动的移动AED（自动体外除颤器）模型，利用城市网约车车队携带AED并将其派遣至院外心脏骤停（OHCA）事发地点。基于纽约和多伦多的真实数据，在AED总量固定的前提下，比较了固定部署、移动部署及混合部署策略的效果。结果显示移动AED可显著改善响应时间和可靠性，但最佳配置比例因城市而异（纽约约55-70%移动配置最优，多伦多则趋向全移动配置），需根据当地覆盖和移动条件量身定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定式AED网络无法适应OHCA需求在时间和空间上的变化，导致及时获取AED成为公共除颤系统的重大瓶颈。作者希望探索一种新的移动AED部署模式，以解决固定网络缺乏适应性的问题。

Method: 提出平台驱动的移动AED模型，利用现有城市网约车车队携带AED并派遣至OHCA事发地点。在保持总AED供应量不变的前提下，比较固定部署、移动部署以及两者混合的多种配置策略。利用纽约市和多伦多的真实城市数据（包括地理参考的公共AED库存、EMS报告的OHCA事件数据以及网约车出行数据）进行分析验证。

Result: 移动AED能显著改善响应时间和可靠性，但效果取决于固定与移动部署之间的容量分配。纽约市平均响应时间改善超过2.5分钟，在约55-70%移动配置时达到最优，超过该范围后改善和可靠性均下降。多伦多平均改善接近4分钟，且随系统趋向全移动配置而趋于稳定。两城市呈现截然不同的模式。

Conclusion: 移动AED能够实质性改善AED的可及性，但固定与移动AED的最佳组合必须根据当地的覆盖条件和移动性状况量身定制，不存在通用的最优配置比例。

Abstract: Timely access to automated external defibrillators (AEDs) remains a major limitation of public-access defibrillation systems because stationary AED networks cannot adapt to spatial and temporal variation in out-of-hospital cardiac arrest (OHCA) demand. We study a platform-enabled mobile AED model in which AEDs are carried by existing urban ride-hailing fleets that can be dispatched to OHCA locations. Rather than assuming additional AED capacity, we hold the total AED supply fixed and compare alternative allocations between stationary and mobile deployment, including hybrid policies that retain both forms of coverage. Our analysis leverages real-world urban data from New York City and Toronto, integrating georeferenced public AED inventories, EMS-reported OHCA incident data, and ride-hailing mobility data. The results show that mobile AEDs can substantially improve both response times and reliability, but the benefits depend on how AED capacity is divided between stationary and mobile deployment. In New York City, average response-time gains exceed 2.5 minutes and peak when approximately 55 to 70 percent of AED capacity is mobile; beyond this range, both gains and reliability decline as stationary coverage is reduced. In Toronto, average gains approach 4 minutes and then plateau as the system moves toward a fully mobile configuration. These contrasting patterns show that mobile AEDs can materially improve access, but the best mix of stationary and mobile AEDs must be tailored to local coverage and mobility conditions.

</details>


### [56] [Dimensionless Controls of Plasticity Under Alternating Tasks: From Evolutionary Biology to Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23889)
*Owen Skriloff*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了环境变化下的可塑性（plasticity），通过一个深度学习最小化模拟系统，将生物学中提出的四种可塑性控制因素重新解释为训练动力学量，发现系统可简化为两个无量纲控制参数：任务分歧度r和可达范围ηT，并推导出可塑性的两个边界，最终通过9,720条轨迹验证了这些发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可塑性在进化生物学和持续学习中都是核心问题。受基因型-表型映射相关工作启发，作者希望探究生物学的可塑性控制因素能否在梯度下降的翻译中存活，从而建立生物学与深度学习之间的联系。

Method: 构建一个最小深度学习模拟系统：网络在两组布尔标签集上交替训练。将四种生物学可塑性因素重新诠释为训练动力学量，将系统简化为两个无量纲控制参数：任务分歧度r（标签不一致的比例）和可达范围ηT（学习率与切换周期的乘积）。通过理论推导获得可塑性的两个边界，并使用ANOVA对9,720条训练轨迹进行统计分析。

Result: 推导出两个上界：r单独决定极值几何地板（utopia distance的几何下限），r和ηT共同约束遗忘。ANOVA证实r、η、T为主导因素，而中性集大小（生物学强调的因素）效应可忽略。最优可达范围遵循近似逆幂律ηT*∝r^-1.18，可仅从任务分歧度得出最优可达范围的启发式规则。

Conclusion: 生物学与深度学习之间存活的类比是动力学层面的而非几何层面的。该框架允许从物理学和工程学中其他驱动系统的视角来审视可塑性，为持续学习提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Plasticity under changing environments is central to both evolutionary biology and continual learning. Motivated by recent work on genotype--phenotype maps, we study a minimal deep-learning analogue where a network is trained alternately on two Boolean label sets, and ask which biological controls of plasticity survive the translation to gradient descent. Reinterpreting four proposed biological factors as quantities of training dynamics, we find the system reduces to two dimensionless controls: the task disagreement $r$, the fraction of disagreeing labels, and the reach $ηT$, the product of learning rate and switching period. We derive two bounds on plasticity: $r$ alone fixes an extremal geometric floor on the utopia distance, while $r$ and $ηT$ jointly bound forgetting. Across 9,720 trajectories, an ANOVA confirms that $r$, $η$, and $T$ dominate, while the effect of neutral-set size (emphasized in the biological setting) is negligible. The optimal reach itself follows an approximate inverse power law $ηT^{*}\propto r^{-1.18}$, yielding a heuristic that sets the optimal reach $ηT^*$ from the task disagreement alone. The analogy that survives is therefore dynamical rather than geometric, and our setting enables a view of plasticity through the lens of other driven systems in physics and engineering.

</details>


### [57] [The Shadow Price of Intelligence: Quality Degradation in LLM Inference as a Supply Chain Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23986)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文论证了在大模型推理服务中，面对算力约束时采用"降级服务"（切小模型、削减推理、截断上下文）的策略在会计上被错误核算。降级导致答案失败，失败产生重试或流失，前者在高峰期放大到达流量，后者摧毁客户终身价值。作者用报童模型、几何重试乘子和两阶段瞬态队列三类经典原语建模，证明静态上更便宜模型在容量紧张时反而消耗更多容量；动态上反应式节流可越过"点火阈值"自造流量，低于降级均衡的释放规则会把瞬态冲击变成永久降级。对异构客户，节流是一个"重试膨胀负载"上的运输问题，按关键比率分等级配给智能，其对偶变量给出了按类别和按小时的智能影子价格。随机分析进一步印证论点：点火边界有预测宽度，噪声惩罚贴边运行的策略。结论：拥塞下节流不是成本杠杆而是需求杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务商普遍以算力受限为由，在拥塞时降级服务（用小模型、压低推理、截断上下文），行业会计认为这节省成本。作者指出该会计错误：它错误地按客户购买答案时的价格来计价一次查询，忽视降级答案会以失败概率导致重试（在系统最忙时放大到达）或流失（摧毁终身价值），从而在系统层面反而增加成本。

Method: 用三类经典排队/库存原语建模推理分配：(1) 报童模型，缺货成本为流失的终身价值；(2) 几何重试乘子，被"回收"的产品是不满意情绪；(3) 两阶段瞬态队列，到达率由重试内生决定。静态分析求解更便宜模型在容量约束下的真实容量消耗；动态分析构建反应式节流微分/差分方程，识别点火阈值与永久降级均衡；对异构客户将问题转化为运输问题（重试膨胀负载）并用关键比率给出最优配给策略及对偶影子价格；再做随机分析刻画点火边界的宽度与噪声影响。

Result: 静态上存在非空可测区域：更便宜模型每满意答案节省能量，但每满意答案消耗严格更多的容量——折扣恰在容量约束生效时反转。动态上，反应式节流在尖峰时可以越过点火阈值，制造比它削减的更多流量；低于降级均衡的释放规则把瞬态尖峰转为永久降级体制。异构客户下最优策略按关键比率分等级配给智能，影子价格可闭式求解（毫秒级），随机分析给出点火边界预测宽度并显示噪声惩罚贴边运行策略。

Conclusion: 拥塞下的节流/降级并非成本杠杆而是需求杠杆：它以"不满意"为再生品制造重试流量并放大流失。正确做法应把降级决策视为按类别按小时配给智能的定价问题，而非单纯的省钱手段；否则折扣策略会在容量真正紧张时产生相反的容量代价，且反应式策略可能因点火效应与随机噪声而自我恶化。

Abstract: Large language model providers are compute constrained, and their universal response to congestion is to degrade service: route queries to smaller models, cut reasoning effort, truncate context. The industry's accounting says this saves money. We show the accounting is wrong, because it prices a query when the customer buys an answer. A degraded answer fails with some probability, and a failed answer either returns as a retry, inflating arrivals when the system is most loaded, or departs as churn, destroying lifetime value on a ledger no cost dashboard displays. We model inference allocation with three classical primitives: a newsvendor whose stockout cost is churned lifetime value, a geometric retry multiplier in which the recycled product is dissatisfaction, and a two-regime transient queue whose arrival rate is made endogenous by retries. Statically, there is a nonempty, measurable regime in which a cheaper model saves energy per satisfied answer while consuming strictly more capacity per satisfied answer, so the discount inverts exactly when capacity binds. Dynamically, a reactive throttle fired during a surge can cross an ignition threshold beyond which it manufactures more traffic than it sheds, and a release rule set below the degraded equilibrium converts a transient surge into a permanent degraded regime. With heterogeneous customers, throttling is a transportation problem in retry-inflated load whose optimal policy rations intelligence by critical ratio, class by class, and whose dual, the shadow price of intelligence, prices a marginal query by class and by hour; closed-form trajectories make it computable in milliseconds. Stochastic analysis sharpens rather than erodes the thesis: the ignition boundary acquires a predicted width, and noise punishes the reactive policy that parks the system against it. Under congestion, throttling is not a cost lever but a demand lever.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of relaxed equilibria for finite-horizon continuous-time time-inconsistent mean field games. We work directly on the product space of relaxed feedback policies and population flows. The policy component is endowed with the stable topology of Young measures, while the population component is restricted to a compact convex set of Wasserstein-continuous flows with uniform moment and time-regularity bounds. For every policy-flow pair, we establish uniform Sobolev and Hölder estimates for the associated auxiliary value function and prove its stability under Young-measure convergence of policies and uniform Wasserstein convergence of population flows. We also establish continuity of the induced population-flow map. The latter requires a duality argument for the Fokker-Planck equations because Young-measure convergence yields only weak-$*$ convergence of the controlled drifts. We then construct a set-valued best-response/consistency map with nonempty compact convex values and apply the Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg fixed-point theorem. The resulting fixed point satisfies both the equilibrium response condition of the intra-personal game and the mean field consistency condition, , thereby establishing the existence of a relaxed equilibrium

Abstract: In this paper, we consider generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) seeking in non-cooperative games with coupled constraint sets. Abstract: We consider solving an optimization instance in which the objective is quadratic and where the decision variable is a probability measure. Our class of problems are motivated by applications arising from optimal transport (with the Gromov-Wasserstein problem being a prominent example) as well as energy landscape minimization. Because the objective depends quadratically on the decision variable, our class of problems fall outside the standard modeling framework of the Generalized Moment Problems (which requires the objective to be linear). To this end, we propose a hierarchy of convex relaxations based on searching over probability measures over products of the base space. These have a natural interpretation with the moment Sum-of-squares hierarchy-a prominent framework for solving polynomial optimization instances, which we adapt to accommodate probability measures. A key conceptual contribution is to introduce a notion of positive-semidefiniteness that extends the usual notion over matrices. Under the assumption that the decision variables satisfy certain marginal constraints (as in the Kantorovich formulation of the optimal transport problem), we establish convergence of our hierarchy towards the globally optimal solution. Under the additional assumption that the objective is a polynomial, we propose a moment-SOS type hierarchy of finite dimensional semidefinite programs whose optimal solution converges to that of the original quadratic optimization over measures. We demonstrate our framework with numerical experiments. More generally, optimization over measures where the objective and/or constraint depends on the decision in a polynomial way is a fundamental problem. It is hoped that our work provides a road-map as to how the ideas of the SOS-ordinarily developed for polynomial optimization-may be applied to a broader class of non-linear problems involving measures.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the absolute value equation (AVE) $Ax-|x|=b$ under residual monotonicity. We characterize this property through the symmetric part of the coefficient matrix, thereby allowing nonsymmetry, and propose a coordinate proximal predictor-corrector (CPPC) method based on an AVE-specific forward-proximal decomposition. An exact scalar proximal update on the largest proximal-residual coordinate generates a predictor point, while a positive-alignment identity certifies the ensuing full-residual separating-hyperplane correction without backtracking. With the current matrix product cached, each iteration requires one new full matrix-vector product. For a nonempty solution set, we prove Fejér monotonicity, whole-sequence convergence, and an $O(K^{-1/2})$ best-iterate residual bound. A positive monotonicity margin further ensures unique solvability for every right-hand side and global linear convergence. Numerical results identify regimes in which the reduced per-iteration work yields shorter solution times.

Abstract: Profitability metrics, such as the single-product gross profit margin, constitute the standard toolbox to prioritize products in sales and operations planning (S&OP). These metrics leverage the premise that production lines can be committed independently, which endows them with a simple per-ton ranking that a planner can compute and use easily in practice. However, coupled plants, especially polymer plants whose parallel lines draw simultaneously on a single bulk feed, can fundamentally undermine this premise and mislead to value-destroying production plans. We propose a framework built on what we call the Augmented Gross Profit for Product Clusters (AGPPC), which converts the per-ton ranking metric into a per-coupled-hour ranking that carries a per-instance optimality certificate. We present the theory of AGPPC, which combines the co-production column concept with a fluid relaxation of the planning problem, alongside an integrated bilinear mixed integer program of the plant and a McCormick-linearized baseline that provides certified bounds for it, and we demonstrate its effectiveness on real operational data from an Indonesian polymer producer, where the coupling-aware metric alone raises operating profit by 7.6% and the full optimization by 28% over current practice.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 建立了最优控制和最优轨迹的存在性；在有限人口时域上证明了至少存在一个均值场博弈均衡，而个体最小时间问题在无限时域上仍然成立。

Abstract: We study a deterministic first-order mean field game model for human capital accumulation in which a continuum of agents invest in education and training to reach a certain qualification level in minimum time. The productivity of learning depends on both the individual's current level of human capital and the distribution of the population, modeling knowledge spillovers and social interactions. Learning is financed by the income generated by existing human capital, leading to a state constraint requiring the financial resources to remain nonnegative throughout the evolution. For a fixed population flow, we formulate the individual problem as a state-constrained minimum-time optimal control problem with time-dependent dynamics. We establish the existence of optimal controls and trajectories, analyze the associated value function through the Dynamic Programming Principle and a Hamilton--Jacobi equation, and characterize the optimal learning effort under suitable regularity and convexity assumptions. The optimal feedback induces a continuity equation describing the evolution of the population distribution. The resulting mean field game is formulated as a fixed-point problem for the population flow. By constructing a compact invariant set and applying Schauder's fixed-point theorem, we prove the existence of at least one equilibrium on every finite population horizon, while the individual minimum-time problem remains formulated on the infinite time horizon.

Abstract: Optimization problems with geometric constraints have a broad range of applications, including machine learning, finance, and control. A powerful algorithmic tool to resolve these geometric constraints are constraint dissolving methods. To this end, we propose a framework for constraint dissolving mappings for nonconvex geometric constraints. Leveraging these, we develop a constraint dissolving inexact penalty method to solve optimization problems with general set-membership constraints and possibly nonconvex geometric constraints. We establish the convergence of the proposed algorithm and prove that every feasible accumulation point is Mordukhovich stationary. Notably, we rely only on mild asymptotic Mordukhovich regularity, which is significantly weaker than the constraint qualifications adopted in the existing literature on constraint dissolving methods. Numerical experiments addressing classical equality-, complementarity-, sparsity-, and low-rank constrained optimization problems demonstrate that the proposed method is competitive with the safeguarded augmented Lagrangian method in terms of solution quality and significantly outperforms the penalty decomposition method.

Abstract: The embedding dimension of categorical predictors is usually selected through heuristic tuning, although it directly affects model complexity, approximation quality, and finite-sample generalization. This paper formulates embedding dimension selection as a constrained allocation problem. The main contribution is to show that embedding capacity can be allocated across heterogeneous categorical predictors according to an explicit approximation-estimation tradeoff. We characterize approximation error through the singular-value tail of the latent category representation, while estimation error increases with total embedding complexity. Under a fixed global embedding budget and a tractable approximation model, this leads to a closed-form allocation rule in which the dimension assigned to each predictor is proportional to the square root of its approximation value relative to its parameter cost. Simulation experiments support the proposed approximation-estimation interpretation and show that the allocation rule improves budget efficiency relative to standard uniform and cardinality-based heuristics, particularly when the budget is binding and predictor heterogeneity is substantial. A real-data healthcare application further shows improvements in predictive accuracy and probabilistic calibration. Overall, the results establish embedding-dimension allocation as a principled finite-sample optimization problem rather than a purely heuristic modeling choice.

</details>

Method: 采用矩匹配（moment-matching）技术，从可用数据集和代价向量的角度推导出LP解有界性的充分条件。在此基础上，进一步讨论了针对非线性系统和多项式特征向量的实用设计方法，为实际应用提供可操作的设计指导。

Conclusion: 通过矩匹配技术为LP形式的无限时域非线性最优控制提供了可验证的有界性保证，填补了该领域理论分析的空白，并为实际实现提供了具体的设计框架。

Abstract: Linear programming (LP) formulations offer a conceptually elegant approach to infinite-horizon, model-free nonlinear optimal control in continuous spaces. However, in addition to the curse of dimensionality, their practical use is limited by the difficulty of consistently obtaining bounded solutions. In this work, we use moment-matching techniques to derive sufficient boundedness conditions in terms of the available dataset and the cost vector of the LP. Moreover, we discuss practical design methods for nonlinear systems and polynomial features.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Caputo分数阶加速梯度下降（CFAGD）方法，适用于光滑和一类非光滑无约束优化问题。该方法结合自适应β^k参数与Caputo分数阶导数，利用非整数阶导数的记忆特性，并通过基于Armijo条件的非精确线搜索选择步长。在强凸性和有界Hessian假设下建立了线性收敛性，数值实验（包括神经网络示例）表明其性能和稳定性优于对比方法。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统整数阶梯度下降方法在优化问题中可能存在收敛速度慢或陷入局部最优的问题。分数阶微积分因其非局部性和记忆特性，有望提供更优的搜索方向和更稳定的收敛行为。然而，现有分数阶优化方法在实际应用中仍面临参数选择困难、适用范围受限等挑战，因此需要一种既适用于光滑目标又适用于非光滑目标、且具有实用参数自适应机制的分数阶加速优化算法。

Method: 该方法的核心思想包括：将Caputo分数阶导数与自适应β^k参数相结合，利用分数阶导数的记忆特性来改善搜索方向；通过启发式更新β^k参数以适应迭代过程；采用基于Armijo条件的非精确线搜索技术选择合适步长，并通过正参数缩放步长以改善迭代点接近最优点时的行为，从而生成下降序列。

Result: 在强凸性和有界Hessian假设下，建立了该方法的线性收敛性。数值验证（包括基于神经网络的示例）表明，CFAGD方法在收敛速度和稳定性方面均优于对比方法，展现出更快的收敛和更稳定的性能表现。

Conclusion: CFAGD方法是一种有效的分数阶加速优化算法，通过引入自适应参数和分数阶导数，在理论和实验层面均验证了其优越性，为分数阶优化方法在更广泛优化问题中的应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: This article proposes a Caputo fractional accelerated gradient descent (CFAGD) method for unconstrained optimization problems that is applicable to both smooth and a class of non-smooth objective functions. The proposed approach incorporates an adaptive (\b{eta}k)-parameter, which is heuristically updated throughout the iterative process to improve the search direction. Furthermore, the method employs the Caputo fractional derivative together with the adaptive (\b{eta}^k)-parameter, thereby preserving the memory characteristics associated with non-integer-order derivatives. A suitable step-size is selected via an inexact line-search technique based on the Armijo condition. The central idea is to scale the step-size by a positive parameter to improve the behavior of the iterates as they approach an optimal point, thereby generating a descent sequence. Under strong convexity and bounded Hessian assumptions, linear convergence of the proposed method is established. Numerical validations, including neural-network-based examples, further indicate that the CFAGD method can achieve faster and more stable performance than competing approaches.

</details>


### [69] [Finite-Sample Metric Non-Collapse for Geometrically Supervised Latent World Models in Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07265)
*Alain Bensoussan,Minh-Nhat Phung,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个用于非线性确定性系统的几何监督潜变量模型的有限样本学习-控制理论。该理论证明：在可计算的单侧正则化条件下，经验风险近似最小化器几乎必然同时具有逐点co-Lipschitz性质和与受控动力学的一致近似半共轭性，从而将近似经验优化转化为度量保真、均匀受控动力学和可靠规划的完整有限样本蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜变量模型学习-控制理论缺乏有限样本保证，特别缺乏将几何监督（仅在训练时使用）与部署时的观测-动作条件化相结合的理论框架。作者旨在弥合"经验优化成功"与"度量保真且动力学受控"之间的理论鸿沟，为可验证的规划提供严格的样本复杂度保证。

Method: 作者引入编码器专用的局部-全局度量铰链（metric hinge），同时强制方向分辨率和分离状态判别。在规则可观测因子、覆盖、有限容量逼近和一致C^{1,1}假设下，建立可计算的单侧正则化机制。通过范数约束的张量积B样条类构造性地实现逼近假设，并利用插值指数将均值残差控制转化为一致界。论文还提供了模块化确定性推论和经验证的有限网结果。

Result: 该理论的核心结果是强选择性质：在高概率下，每个近似经验最小化器同时是逐点co-Lipschitz的且均匀近似半共轭于受控动力学。逼近、采样和优化误差被显式区分。插值指数是尖锐的。受控实验隔离了塌缩与折叠现象，量化了解析证书的余量，并证明了恢复度量分辨率的控制收益。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是建立了一个完整的有限样本蕴含链条：从近似经验最优化到度量保真、均匀受控动力学和同一学习模型的可靠规划。这为几何监督潜变量模型提供了可验证的、具有明确样本复杂度的端到端学习-控制理论保证。

Abstract: We establish a finite-sample learning-to-control theory for geometrically supervised latent models of nonlinear deterministic systems. Geometric supervision is used only during training: simulator state, proprioception, or state estimates with independently validated metric and directional error bounds supply observable-state distances and tangent directions, while deployment remains observation- and action-conditioned. We introduce an encoder-only local--global metric hinge that enforces directional resolution and separated-state discrimination. Under regular observable-factor, coverage, finite-capacity approximation, and uniform $C^{1,1}$ hypotheses, a computable one-sided regularization regime has a strong selection property: with high probability, every approximate empirical minimizer is simultaneously pointwise co-Lipschitz and uniformly approximately semiconjugate to the controlled dynamics. Approximation, sampling, and optimization errors remain explicit and separate. Norm-constrained tensor-product B-spline classes constructively realize the approximation hypotheses, and the interpolation exponent converting mean residual control into a uniform bound is sharp. A modular deterministic corollary transfers the learned certificates to trajectory, finite-horizon cost, learned-cost-head, and optimizer guarantees, while a validated finite-net result enables sharper model-specific certification. Controlled experiments isolate collapse and folding, quantify the analytic certificate's reserve, and demonstrate the control benefit of restored metric resolution. The principal contribution is a complete finite-sample implication from approximate empirical optimization to metric faithfulness, uniform controlled dynamics, and reliable planning for the same learned model.

</details>

### [70] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDER基准，通过固定对话内容而改变读者可见的工件（memory条目、摘要、类型化记录、原始对话片段），系统性地研究RAG/记忆评估中答案模型输入形式对性能的影响。实验表明，匹配预算的解析包比按recency截断的原始对话高42.4-72.6分，且在多种模板和模型间表现出显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Memory和RAG评估通常将答案模型的输入视为实现细节，但相同历史内容以不同形式（记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录、原始摘录）呈现时，模型的检索和推理能力可能截然不同。缺乏对这种"读者可见工件"影响的系统控制。

Method: RENDER基准包含两个核心组件：(1) 五级"数据包阶梯"（packet ladder），用于定位包含答案的内容进入输入的时机；(2) 确定性模板，近似模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上进行评估，并使用judge重新评分验证，同时在不同检索噪声和HotpotQA上检验迁移性。

Result: 匹配预算的数据包比按recency截断的原始对话高出42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板中每个模型的best-worst差距为24.6-48.8分；主要评分器下ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中的7个上优于原始对话；judge重评分保留正向聚合效应但模型级显著性混合；3个在形式化ledger数据包上得分为0的模型在自然语言条目上达到45.4-53.4%准确率；效应在检索噪声下依然存在并迁移至HotpotQA。

Conclusion: Memory/RAG评估应报告或控制读者可见的工件形式，因为同一历史内容以不同形式呈现会导致巨大的性能差异。建议评估基准在构建时将呈现格式视为需明确声明和控制的变量，而非实现细节。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [71] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个面向企业Oracle环境的NL2SQL基准，包含系统化的复杂性分层和静默分歧评估。通过465张表、164,682行数据的六个填充模式，以及550个黄金验证的问题-查询对，揭示现有基准（Spider/BIRD）高执行精度与企业实际场景之间的巨大差距。GPT-4o schema-linked随层级单调退化（79.8/60.3/57.2 EX），EM全程低于7%，静默分歧在EX通过查询中高达73-99%；Claude Sonnet 4.6在每层均超越GPT-4o。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider和BIRD）声称执行准确率超过89%，但这些基准依赖简化的学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的真实复杂性。需要建立面向企业Oracle环境的、具备系统复杂性分层和静默分歧评估的基准，以更准确地衡量现代NL2SQL模型的实际表现。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench：1) Oracle优先的NL2SQL基准，设置三个企业模式复杂性层级；2) 发布六个填充模式（465表、164,682行、无空表），在Oracle/PostgreSQL/MySQL/SQL Server四种数据库上保持相同种子数据；3) 建立四指标评估框架（EM、EX、SR、SD），其中SD为静默分歧指标；4) 构建550个黄金验证的问题-查询对（Tier-1:95, Tier-2:228, Tier-3:227）；5) 评估GPT-4o（schema-linked与zero-shot）和Claude Sonnet 4.6及本地Llama 3.2的性能。

Result: GPT-4o schema-linked的EX跨阶层单调退化：79.8/60.3/57.2（执行查询上），而早期142题试点切片为75.6/80.4/95.8（趋势反转）。EM在所有阶层低于7%；EX通过查询中的静默分歧达73-99%。错误分析显示高层级中错误结果语义占主导。Claude Sonnet 4.6 schema-linked达87.4/74.9/68.7 EX，全面超过GPT-4o。GPT-4o zero-shot在Tier 2-3上反转（78.7/73.5/77.8），归因于较低执行率和幸存者偏差。Llama 3.2仅13.3% EX，凸显闭源API模型与开源基线的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 企业级Oracle模式下的NL2SQL性能远低于现有学术基准所暗示的水平。高静默分歧率（73-99%）意味着即便查询执行成功，其结果语义也可能错误，这对实际企业应用构成严重风险。当前闭源模型（Claude/GPT-4o）与开源模型（Llama 3.2）之间的性能差距显著，提示开源NL2SQL在企业场景中仍有很长的路要走。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [72] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，将LLM与高保真科学仿真模型耦合，使LLM代理能够对制药工艺设计中的系统进行受控实验。系统通过结构化任务表示、实验设计、比较仿真和结果解读，生成基于证据的工艺参数优化建议，相比纯语言推理产生更具体、更可操作的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与工程任务不仅需要LLM生成看似合理的文本和代码，更需要理解系统对干预的响应机制，而这依赖于受控实验。当前LLM缺少与真实物理仿真模型交互的能力，导致难以提供可靠的工艺优化建议。

Method: 提出多智能体框架，包含以下关键模块：1) 从用户查询和基线配置构建结构化任务表示；2) 设计受控实验方案；3) 执行比较性仿真实验；4) 解读仿真结果；5) 综合证据形成工艺参数优化建议。通过将LLM与高保真仿真模型在交互式代理框架中耦合，支持基于干预、比较和观察的推理链。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该方法相比纯语言推理模式产生更高特异性的输出，并在用户评分的正确性和有用性方面表现更优。消融研究和可视化案例分析进一步验证了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 将语言模型与高保真仿真模型耦合的多智能体框架，能够使LLM超越文本生成限制，通过受控实验进行科学推理，在制药工艺设计等工业场景中产生更具体、更可操作且用户认可度更高的优化建议。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning.
